# RECEIPT POOLS — THE DOSSIER STATE-PROSE CORPUS (the settlement explaining its standing condition)

## Authored 2026-08-03 for DESIGN_FP_SPINE.md §2, SP-6's **THE DOSSIER-NATIVE
## REGISTER** and its amendment **THE STATE-PROSE CORPUS + THE FOREIGN DESK OF
## THE TOWN** (owner order, same day). Sibling files:
## RECEIPT_POOLS_CAUSAL_DOSSIER.md (the same register applied to causal JOINS —
## its §0b register, §0c slots, §0d bands and §0f resolutions are the parent
## conventions this file inherits) and RECEIPT_POOLS_CAUSAL.md (the Herald's
## three registers). This annex is CONTENT, not architecture — it invents no
## mechanism, re-opens no ruling, adds no coupling, mints no engine state, mints
## no receipt kind, and changes no rendered digit.

**AS MERGED (VERIFIER-1, 2026-08-03).** Four clusters, **59 blocks, 2,149
authored variants**, **coverage 77/77** against the surface census in §0g.
Section order is WRITER-1 → WRITER-4; the writer markers are kept because each
carries cluster-scoped resolutions, and **block ids, not file position, are the
addressing scheme**. Every merge decision is recorded in §0h and is vetoable.

**What this file is.** The dossier-native register applied not to joins but to
**STANDING STATE**. The sibling annex answers *why is the town like this*; this
file answers the prior question the dossier never actually asked in prose —
*what is the town like, said in the town's own voice*. Every closed band,
status and posture the dossier renders gets explanatory prose written as the
settlement explaining its own condition, so that a page stops being a wall of
enum words at 22px with a bare `NN/100` beside them.

The owner's two calibration sentences are the ceiling and the floor of the
register, unchanged from the sibling:

> *"The market runs thin; the routes turned east after the war and have not come back."*
>
> *"The granary stands half-empty — the bill of the tribute year, still being paid."*

Both sentences carry a cause because their records carry one. **The hardest law
in this file is that most state does not.** A prosperity rung is a derived
reading with no provenance trail behind it; a prose variant that supplies one
is inventing history. See §0f R-DST-B.

---

## §0a SCOPE

**IN scope.** Section FRAMING and state EXPLANATION for every dossier surface
whose content is a closed band, status, posture or tier — economy, supply
links and breaks, power, defense, stressors and conditions, faith, war footing,
population, relations. Per state-shape: the driving fields, the slots, and 3–5
angle-distinct native-voice variants per meaningful state-value combination.

**OUT of scope, explicitly.**

- **HOOK CONTENT.** The adventure hooks themselves — their text, their
  selection, their non-redundancy — are owned by the **HK program** (HK-1..3)
  and its annexes. This corpus covers the hooks section's **FRAMING** only:
  the sentence that says what kind of situation the town is currently offering,
  never a hook.
- **CAUSAL JOINS.** A sentence whose subject is one domain's effect on another
  belongs to RECEIPT_POOLS_CAUSAL_DOSSIER.md's join families. This file's
  sentences are about a state, not about a link.
- **ENGINE-AUTHORED STRINGS.** `situationDesc`, `dependency.impact`,
  `entrepotNote`, `magicNote`, `economicDragDesc`, `upstreamNote` and their
  siblings are generator prose. This corpus FRAMES them; it never restates
  them, and a composer that prints both must not say the same thing twice.

**The fences carry over unchanged from the sibling annex §0a:** truth-side
sources only, no rumor/belief/disinfo ledger as a content source, the truth
law, fail-closed audience projection (J-CPL-5), and **LAW ONE** — the divine is
never confirmed. Where a state is held up by a temple's observance, the prose
reports the observance and the effect and never the god's act.

**Composition lives display-side.** Nothing here is state. Dark ⇒ the page is
byte-identical.

## §0b THE REGISTER — inherited whole

| It is | It is not |
|---|---|
| present tense, describing a condition that STANDS | past-tense narration of an event |
| the state first, the cause second **where a cause exists** | the cause first with the state as its payoff |
| one flowing sentence, or two short ones | a paragraph, a cascade, or a list |
| woven — readable as the third sentence of the section it sits in | a headline, a standfirst, a pull-quote, a panel |
| the town's own voice about the town | a desk's voice about a town |

**THE WOVEN TEST** (sibling R-DOS-D, adopted): a variant passes only if it can
be dropped into the middle of its section without a seam. Forbidden outright:
leading with a typed action verb; the headline's single em-dash turn used as
the sentence's whole architecture; any desk or dateline gesture. An em-dash is
lawful as *apposition inside* a woven sentence.

**THE ANGLE PALETTE.** Variants inside a pool must be angle-distinct; two
variants differing only in slot fills are ONE for the repetition envelope. The
sibling's six angles, plus one minted here for state prose:

| Angle | The standpoint |
|---|---|
| `[ledger]` | the clerk's view — what the books, rolls and counts show |
| `[street]` | the town's own talk about its condition |
| `[visitor]` | what a stranger notices first, without being told |
| `[elder]` | the memory frame — the condition as something with a length to it |
| `[unfolding]` | the movement still running, not yet settled |
| `[counterforce]` | the thing that did NOT happen — the cap, the prop, the restraint, plainly stated |
| `[threshold]` **(new)** | the band's own edge — the state is what it is *and* what would tip it, stated without predicting that it will |

`[threshold]` exists because a band is a range, and the difference between the
top and the bottom of one is exactly what a DM needs and what an enum word
destroys. It states an edge; it never forecasts a crossing.

## §0c SLOTS

Every proper noun is a slot; nothing is baked.

| Slot | Fills with |
|---|---|
| `{settlement}` | **the town whose page this is** (sibling R-DOS-A — always the page's own town) |
| `{counterpart}` | the other end of a directed relation |
| `{faction}` | the acting faction, seat, bloc or house |
| `{institution}` | a named building or house on the settlement's roster |
| `{npc}` | a cast person named on a member receipt; never minted, no fate resolved |
| `{good}` | the named trade good |
| `{resource}` | the named raw input |
| `{chain}` | the supply chain by its own label |
| `{route}` | the named road, lane, crossing or pass |
| `{access}` | the trade-access word rendered in prose form: *the road · the river · the port · the crossroads · the pass* (`isolated` has no `{access}` fill — a variant needing one is not offered on an isolated town) |
| `{complexity}` | the generator's `economicComplexity` string, passed through as a noun phrase |
| `{season}` | the stockpile record's own season word |
| `{band}` | a band word from a closed vocabulary — never a figure |
| `{reason}` | the RECORDED reason, typed, from the field's own reason slot |
| `{timeband_*}` | see §0d |

### §0c-2 CLUSTER-MINTED SLOTS — the merged register

*Each cluster minted the proper nouns §0c does not carry. They are collected
here so the file has ONE slot register; the minting rulings stay where they were
written (WRITER-2's `SLOTS MINTED BY THIS CLUSTER`, WRITER-3's `W3-a`, and the
per-block declarations of WRITER-4). Nothing is minted here.*

| Slot | Fills with | Minted by |
|---|---|---|
| `{seat}` | the governing body **by its own generated name** — tier-scaled (*Headman's Authority*, *Town Council*, *Grand Merchant Oligarchy*); never a baked noun | WRITER-2 |
| `{term}` | a treaty term by its own family label (**declared shared** with RECEIPT_POOLS_WAR.md) | WRITER-2 |
| `{creed}` | the deity or faith by its authored name | WRITER-3 |
| `{rival_creed}` | the opposing, rising or contesting creed | WRITER-3 |
| `{faction2}` | the second party on a two-party record (a tension, a conflict, a `faction_split` clock) | WRITER-4 |
| `{issue}` | the conflict record's own `issue` string | WRITER-4 |
| `{stakes}` | the conflict record's own `stakes` string — offered **only** where the field is non-empty | WRITER-4 |
| `{govFaction}` | the governing faction named on the temple-economy coherence note | WRITER-4 |
| `{governing}` | the governing power as the legitimacy clock's framing names it | WRITER-4 |
| `{controller}` | the holder of a hook's controlling interest, where the hook record names one | WRITER-4 |
| `{steading}` | a satellite steading by its own name | WRITER-4 |
| `{ruin}` | the ancient ruin by its own name | WRITER-4 |
| `{event}` | a historical event by its recorded name | WRITER-4 |
| `{founder}` | `founding.foundedBy` | WRITER-4 |
| `{challenge}` | `founding.initialChallenge` | WRITER-4 |

**THE SLOTS-LINE CONVENTION, RECONCILED (§0h V1-h).** WRITER-2 declares each
block's **permitted palette**; the other three declare the **used set**. The
merged rule is the permissive one: *a slot used in a variant MUST appear on its
block's SLOTS line; a slot declared need not be used.* A selector may therefore
read a SLOTS line as the fill contract for the block, and a walker may check the
first half of that rule and not the second.

## §0d TIME BANDS (closed) AND QUANTITY BANDS (closed)

**No digits appear in dossier-state prose, ever.** Durations render through the
six-band table (sibling §0d, adopted unchanged):

| Band | attributive `{timeband}` | span `{timeband_span}` | adverbial `{timeband_since}` | predicate `{timeband_age}` |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| this season | this season's | this season | this season | of this season |
| within the year | the year's | the year | within the year | not yet a year old |
| years on | years' | years | years on | years old |
| a decade's | a decade's | a decade | a decade on | a decade old |
| a generation's | a generation's | a generation | a generation on | a generation old |
| older than its bearers | — | — | — | older than its bearers |

*"Older than its bearers"* is PREDICATE-ONLY.

**Counts** speak only in `QUANTITY_BANDS` words (`a few souls`, `a dozen or so`,
`dozens`, `a hundred or so`, `several hundred`, `many hundreds`, `thousands`,
`nobody`) or in an authored magnitude word (`a handful`, `a score`, `most`,
`all but`). **Proportions** speak in words (`a corner of it`, `a real portion`,
`well past`, `barely`), never in percent.

This bans the corpus from echoing what the surfaces themselves print: `NN/100`,
`Deficit N%`, `NN% capture`, `N% recovery`, `N / N lbs/day`, `N.N of N.N
months`. The digit stays on the tile; the prose bands the same fact. See
R-DST-D.

## §0e AUDIENCE

Every variant is DM-truth by default (the dossier is the DM's page). A variant
whose content is **covert** — off-book capture, a faction's rents, a bypassed
blockade, anything the town itself does not admit — carries **`AUDIENCE:
dm-only`** inline as `· dm-only` in its angle tag. The player-side projection
**truncates to silence**, never to a hint: a page over a state with a covert
seam must be byte-identical to a page over a state that genuinely lacks one.

Premium-gated content follows the same marking discipline; a gated variant is
absent, not teased.

## §0f AUTHORING RESOLUTIONS (recorded, vetoable — the chair's, not the implementer's)

- **R-DST-A — SURFACE blocks and LADDER blocks are non-redundant by
  construction.** A `DS-*` block whose STATE-KEY is a rendered *surface* (a
  header, a tile row, a card, a panel) authors that surface's **framing** — the
  sentence the section needs about itself. A block whose STATE-KEY is a closed
  *ladder* authors the **band value's own reading**. A composed page draws at
  most one of each per section and never both about the same fact. Where the
  two touch, the block says so and names its sibling.
- **R-DST-B — STRUCTURAL cause and HISTORICAL cause are different licences, and
  this is the file's central law.** A standing *configuration* field
  (`tradeAccess`, `isEntrepot`, `resourceCondition: absent`, a tier, a rung)
  licenses a **structural** clause — *what the town is arranged like* ("nothing
  reaches it but over the pass"). It NEVER licenses a **historical** clause —
  *what happened to the town* ("the road closed in the war"). Only an
  event-provenance field licenses a historical clause: `upstreamNote`,
  `magicNote` + `magicRecovery`, `severingCause` + weeks cut,
  `stockpile.blockaded` / `.blockadeBypass`, `regionalPressures[]`,
  `dependency.{resource,impact}`, `entrepotNote`, and the reconciliation log.
  **A prosperity rung, a food label, a posture word and a market band carry NO
  provenance whatsoever** and get plain description only. This is where an
  unchecked writer invents a war.
- **R-DST-C — the frozen copy units are NOT replaced.** `PROSPERITY_READINGS`,
  `FOOD_SECURITY_LEVELS` readings, `CHAIN_STATUS_LEVELS` readings, `BAND_COPY`
  headlines, `CRIER_FRAMES`, `NEED_HEURISTICS` consequences, the market-prices
  footnote and `ECONOMY_FRESHNESS_SENTENCES` are existing authored strings with
  their own pins and their own vetoes. This corpus **surrounds** them. Where an
  existing string is already in register, it is recorded as **canonical-at-zero**
  (index 0 of its pool, per the `CRIER_FRAMES` precedent) and the corpus adds
  angle-distinct siblings for variance; where it is not, the corpus adds prose
  beside it and touches it not at all. Nothing here is a rewrite request.
- **R-DST-D — the digit ban versus the surfaces that print digits.** Stated in
  §0d. The tile keeps its `NN/100`; the prose says *a broad margin*. A composer
  that prints both must not let the prose restate the number, because the
  number can move without the band moving and the pair would then disagree.
- **R-DST-E — magnitude is a two-band read on any prop, and the pools must not
  read alike across it.** `magicRecovery` was carried forward in Wave 8 exactly
  so a quarter-prop and a near-total rescue stop being indistinguishable
  downstream; prose that flattens them undoes the fix in the one place the
  reader actually looks. Every prop pool is authored twice, small and large.
- **R-DST-F — the `stable` fallthrough is a HAZARD, and the `stable` pool is
  written defensively.** `canonicalSupplyChainStatus` maps an unknown status to
  `stable`, so a bare status word is not evidence of health. Every `stable` /
  `Running` variant is authored to be false-sounding if a note field is present
  ("nothing in it is being covered for"), and the selector contract is: **draw
  the health pool only when the chain's note fields are empty.** A composer
  that ignores this will print "the line holds" over a propped, depleted chain.
- **R-DST-G — the good states get prose as rich as the broken ones.** Owner
  instruction, enforced here as an authoring quota: every ladder's top rungs
  carry the same variant count as its bottom rungs, and no healthy pool is
  allowed to be a single sentence of relief. A thriving market is a subject,
  not the absence of one.
- **R-DST-H — a "no producer yet" status is authored anyway, and marked.**
  `captured` and `collapsing` are defined in the canonical vocabulary with no
  generator producing them. Their pools are written and tagged **NO PRODUCER —
  do not wire a selector until a producer exists**, so that the day one lands
  the prose is not improvised.
- **R-DST-I — a quoted in-world VOICE and the page's own narration are never
  merged.** The market crier speaks in quotation marks and is a different
  register; the dossier's narration frames the cry and never imitates it. Every
  market-prices variant here is a frame, not a cry.
- **R-DST-J — where an engine receipt counts, the prose bands.** The starvation
  receipt prints *"no shipment in 4 weeks"* by design — it is a DM receipt, not
  this register. The prose beside it says `{timeband_since}`. The two are never
  concatenated into one sentence, or the page bans and prints a digit in the
  same breath.
- **R-DST-K — a band that is not rendered gets NO prose.** Seasons off ⇒ no
  granary tile ⇒ no granary sentence. No measured flow ⇒ `null` drift ⇒ no
  drift sentence. Below the shadow-economy floor ⇒ no section ⇒ no sentence.
  Silence is the correct output, and a composer that reaches for a "nothing to
  report" line has invented a state the engine does not hold.
- **R-DST-L — frequency and repetition are per-settlement windowed** (sibling
  R-DOS-H). *A town should not explain itself in the same words as its
  neighbour.* The envelope covers these pools at the BLOCK grain, per settlement
  per season window.

---

## §0g THE COVERAGE CENSUS — 77 SURFACES, AND WHO OWNS EACH

**The denominator is the dossier itself.** Finite semantics makes the census
total: every dossier surface whose content is a closed band, status, posture or
tier inside the ten domains the owner order names (economy · supply links and
breaks · power · hooks framing · defense · stressors and conditions · faith ·
war footing · population · relations). **The grain is one row per RENDERED
SURFACE** — a section, a banner, a panel, a header, a card group — and **not**
one row per ladder: a closed vocabulary is a *vocabulary inside* a surface, and
the ladder blocks (`DS-ECO-8`, `DS-ECO-9`, `DS-SUP-2`, `DS-ECO-10`, `DS-STR-2`,
`DS-CND-1`, `DS-FTH-3`, `DS-POP-2`, `DS-POW-6`, `DS-DEF-10`, `DS-WAR-5`) appear
